No installation, no API keys, no configuration.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTip for Codex users:\u003c/strong\u003e You do not need to connect an extra API to customize this tool. Open this repo in Codex and point it at this document or the codebase, then describe what you want to change. Codex can update the software directly.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis is a combination of dynamic software and a no-UI workflow: instead of operating fixed controls inside an interface, you describe the tool or workflow you need, and the agent changes the tool to match.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor durable agent instructions and prompt templates, see \u003ca href=\"/NO6KIKO/gorest-2d-animation-spritesheet-generator/blob/main/AGENTS.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"/NO6KIKO/gorest-2d-animation-spritesheet-generator/blob/main/docs/AGENT_WORKFLOW.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003edocs/AGENT_WORKFLOW.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRecommended model:\u003c/strong\u003e use GPT-5.5 (\u003ccode\u003egpt-5.5\u003c/code\u003e) for this workflow. 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe app is designed for fast visual iteration: open a scene, place the character and props, inspect every spritesheet used in that scene, tune metadata, preview movement, and save the finished setup.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCodex-Assisted Animation Agent\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-codex-assisted-animation-agent\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Codex-Assisted Animation Agent\" href=\"#codex-assisted-animation-agent\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe core idea is to vibe-code a no-manual-pipeline 2D animation agent without relying on a video generation model.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInstead of manually operating a traditional animation stack, you can prompt Codex, upload a reference image, and let Codex call the local agent to produce a game-ready 2D spritesheet. The app then plays the animation directly from that spritesheet in a live preview, so the result can be checked immediately and used in a game engine.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe workflow is broader than character animation:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCodex can generate or import spritesheets.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCodex can place animated sprites onto a background like a scene-compositing tool.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCodex can configure layers, metadata, trigger settings, scene state, and preview behavior.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCodex can revise the product framework itself while the tool evolves.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe browser becomes the visual result surface, while Codex handles setup, editing, and iteration.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis is meant to reduce or skip the manual Spine-style workflow for early game prototyping: prompt, generate, composite, preview, adjust, and save.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat You Can Do\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-you-can-do\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What You Can Do\" href=\"#what-you-can-do\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCreate and save multiple scenes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eArrange scene cards on the \u003ccode\u003e2D Canvas\u003c/code\u003e as a scrollable freeform flow board.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse scene-card shortcuts on the \u003ccode\u003e2D Canvas\u003c/code\u003e: \u003ccode\u003eCtrl+C\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eCtrl+V\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eCtrl+D\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eCtrl+X\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eDelete\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eBackspace\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eCtrl+Z\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eCtrl+Y\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRight-click scene cards for copy, cut, paste, duplicate, and delete actions.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePlace backgrounds, characters, props, UI elements, and animated sprites as editable layers.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDrag, resize, scale, reorder, hide, and show scene layers.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePreview a playable side-scroller scene with character movement.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eImport or generate 12-frame and 16-frame spritesheets.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eComposite animated sprites directly onto scene backgrounds.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOpen the \u003ccode\u003eSpritesheets\u003c/code\u003e page to see every animation clip used by the current scene.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEdit spritesheet metadata such as asset role, clip name, trigger type, game state, direction, loop mode, and tags.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLet Codex configure scenes, layers, assets, and animation metadata from prompts.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLet Codex modify the tool's product framework as the workflow changes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSave reusable assets and scenes into the local game asset library.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" PowerShell:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"$env:PORT=3001\nnpm run dev\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003e$\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eenv:\u003c/span\u003ePORT\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e3001\u003c/span\u003e\nnpm run dev\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThen open \u003ccode\u003ehttp://localhost:3001\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCreator Workflow\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-creator-workflow\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Creator Workflow\" href=\"#creator-workflow\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis project is organized around a game-scene authoring workflow rather than a single character animation tool. A typical production pass looks like this:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eStart from the \u003ccode\u003eScenes\u003c/code\u003e tab.\nCreate a new scene, duplicate an existing one, delete test scenes, or open a saved scene from the scene library. The \u003ccode\u003e2D Canvas\u003c/code\u003e view works as a freeform scene-flow board, so cards can be moved, resized, copied, pasted, duplicated, cut, deleted, and undone independently from layer edits inside an individual scene.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBuild the scene base in the \u003ccode\u003eScene\u003c/code\u003e tab.\nAdd a background first, then place the player character, props, UI objects, foreground objects, animated props, and effects as separate editable layers.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eArrange layers like a 2D composition.\nDrag objects on the canvas, resize them with handles, adjust scale, and use the layer list to decide what appears above or below other objects.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eKeep animation assets reusable.\nSave finished characters, props, UI elements, and effects into the local asset library so the same asset can be inserted into future scenes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInspect all animation clips in \u003ccode\u003eSpritesheets\u003c/code\u003e.\nThe \u003ccode\u003eSpritesheets\u003c/code\u003e page lists every spritesheet used in the current scene, not just the main character. Use it to preview, locate, download, and edit each animation clip.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTune each clip as game data.\nEach spritesheet can carry metadata such as action name, direction, loop mode, trigger type, trigger value, game state, and tags. This makes the visual asset reusable as gameplay data later.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePreview the scene in \u003ccode\u003eAction\u003c/code\u003e.\nUse the playable preview to check character movement, animation timing, layer order, lighting, scale, and scene composition.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSave the completed scene.\nSave the scene once the background, character, props, spritesheets, and metadata feel correct.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePrompt Examples for Codex\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-prompt-examples-for-codex\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Prompt Examples for Codex\" href=\"#prompt-examples-for-codex\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThese prompts are written for Codex. They describe the scene, the spritesheet, the visual mood, and the exact editor changes Codex should make.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Create a new side-scroller scene in the asset library. The scene should be a refined Chinese-horror Shinto-style wooden hut interior with a long wooden table in the foreground. Add a separate animated prop layer on the table: a round glass fishbowl spritesheet with a realistic fantail goldfish swimming inside. Use a dark, polished, painterly horror mood, not a simple cartoon style. Save the background PNG, the spritesheet PNG, and wire the new scene into public/generated/game_asset_library.json.\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eCreate a new side-scroller scene in the asset library. The scene should be a refined Chinese-horror Shinto-style wooden hut interior with a long wooden table in the foreground. Add a separate animated prop layer on the table: a round glass fishbowl spritesheet with a realistic fantail goldfish swimming inside. Use a dark, polished, painterly horror mood, not a simple cartoon style. Save the background PNG, the spritesheet PNG, and wire the new scene into public/generated/game_asset_library.json.\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Generate a 16-frame transparent PNG spritesheet for a tabletop prop. The prop is a round glass fishbowl with murky water, glass highlights, plants, dark sediment, and a realistic fancy goldfish. The fish should have an egg-shaped body, high dorsal fin, orange-white scales, and split flowing tail fins. Keep every frame 512 x 512, arrange it as a 4 x 4 sheet, and make the swim loop subtle and game-ready.\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGenerate a 16-frame transparent PNG spritesheet for a tabletop prop. The prop is a round glass fishbowl with murky water, glass highlights, plants, dark sediment, and a realistic fancy goldfish. The fish should have an egg-shaped body, high dorsal fin, orange-white scales, and split flowing tail fins. Keep every frame 512 x 512, arrange it as a 4 x 4 sheet, and make the swim loop subtle and game-ready.\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Revise the current fishbowl spritesheet so it looks less like a simple icon. Research real goldfish references first, then redraw it toward a realistic fantail goldfish direction. Keep the bowl round, preserve transparent background, update frame metadata, and adjust the scene layer position so the fishbowl sits naturally on the wooden table.\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eRevise the current fishbowl spritesheet so it looks less like a simple icon. Research real goldfish references first, then redraw it toward a realistic fantail goldfish direction. Keep the bowl round, preserve transparent background, update frame metadata, and adjust the scene layer position so the fishbowl sits naturally on the wooden table.\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Create a new scene card in the 2D Canvas for a horizontal horror shrine entrance. The background should show a Shinto-style gate entrance with a foreground incense burner. Add a talking character close-up spritesheet as a separate layer, configure the active animation, and make sure the scene appears in the scene flow board with editable metadata.\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eCreate a new scene card in the 2D Canvas for a horizontal horror shrine entrance. The background should show a Shinto-style gate entrance with a foreground incense burner. Add a talking character close-up spritesheet as a separate layer, configure the active animation, and make sure the scene appears in the scene flow board with editable metadata.\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSpritesheet Authoring Rules\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-spritesheet-authoring-rules\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Spritesheet Authoring Rules\" href=\"#spritesheet-authoring-rules\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor stable 2D game animation, spritesheets should be treated as one complete animation sheet first, then split into frames. This avoids the common problem where individually generated frames change size, drift, or lose visual consistency.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRecommended rules:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eGenerate or import a full spritesheet whenever possible, then split it into frames.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eKeep every frame the same pixel size.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eKeep the character or prop centered in a consistent frame box.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePreserve the original character proportions from the reference image.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eKeep the feet or base anchor stable so the object does not slide around during playback.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse small, close frame-to-frame changes for idle and breathing animations.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse one clean walk cycle per sheet, then loop it in playback instead of repeating the same step too many times inside the sheet.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor side-scrollers, create separate clips for \u003ccode\u003eidle\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ewalk_right\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ewalk_left\u003c/code\u003e, and any special action.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eStore direction, loop, trigger, and game-state metadata on the clip so it can be reused by scene logic.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eInteraction Design Model\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-interaction-design-model\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Interaction Design Model\" href=\"#interaction-design-model\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInteractive scenes are easiest to manage when interaction behavior is separated from visual layers. The recommended structure is:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInteraction zone: a draggable and resizable area that decides where the player can interact.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTrigger condition: proximity, mouse click, keyboard input, inventory requirement, or scene-state requirement.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAction: show text, play an animation, hide or show a layer, give an item, switch scenes, or update game state.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eState: a small key-value table such as \u003ccode\u003ehas_key\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eradio_on\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003edoor_open\u003c/code\u003e, or \u003ccode\u003evisited_bedroom\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUseful presets for future scene building:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eInspect\u003c/code\u003e: show an eye prompt near an object, then display text when clicked.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePickup\u003c/code\u003e: collect an item and hide the scene object.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eToggle\u003c/code\u003e: switch a layer, animation, or state on and off.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eDoor / Scene Link\u003c/code\u003e: move from one scene to another.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAnimated Prop\u003c/code\u003e: play or loop a prop animation.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eConditional\u003c/code\u003e: choose different results based on scene state or inventory.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eScene Framing\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-scene-framing\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Scene Framing\" href=\"#scene-framing\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe editor supports a scene-size mindset instead of only a single image-size mindset. Use this when designing for different targets:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSet the full scene width and height for long side-scrolling spaces.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSet the viewport frame separately for desktop, tablet, or phone-style previews.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePosition the background inside the viewport to test what the player sees first.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eKeep gameplay objects in world coordinates so the player can move through a larger scene.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse foreground and background layers to create depth while keeping the game readable.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eIncluded Scene Flow Example\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-included-scene-flow-example\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Included Scene Flow Example\" href=\"#included-scene-flow-example\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe current sample library includes a horror Shinto shrine entrance scene for a side-scroller prototype. It uses a wide shrine-gate background with a foreground incense burner, a talking Wei Yang close-up spritesheet, and editable layer metadata so the scene can be adjusted directly in the browser.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIt also includes a Shinto-style wooden hut interior scene with a refined horror tabletop fishbowl spritesheet. The fishbowl is stored as a reusable animated prop, so it can be inspected from the \u003ccode\u003eSpritesheets\u003c/code\u003e page and reused in other scenes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSpritesheet Page\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-spritesheet-page\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Spritesheet Page\" href=\"#spritesheet-page\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003eSpritesheets\u003c/code\u003e page is a scene-level animation library. It lists each spritesheet clip currently attached to scene layers, including character animations, animated props, UI animations, and imported assets.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEach card supports:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePreview\u003c/code\u003e: play that clip in the frame preview.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eLocate Layer\u003c/code\u003e: jump back to the scene editor with the matching layer selected.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePNG\u003c/code\u003e: download the underlying spritesheet image when available.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eEdit\u003c/code\u003e: change metadata for reusable confirmed assets.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBuilt-in scene kit assets can be previewed and layered. Persistent metadata editing is available for confirmed user assets.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eGame Asset Library\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-game-asset-library\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Game Asset Library\" also stored in:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"public/generated/\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003epublic/generated/\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis makes the project easy to keep as a self-contained prototype. class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Gorest 2D Animation Spritesheet Generator</h1><a id="user-content-gorest-2d-animation-spritesheet-generator" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Gorest 2D Animation Spritesheet Generator" href="#gorest-2d-animation-spritesheet-generator"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">An open-source, no-UI 2D game and animation generative platform for turning concepts, references, and spritesheets into animation-ready scenes, previews, and reusable game assets.</p>
<p dir="auto">We open-sourced this 2D platform to make game creation simpler, and we will keep improving it in public. We welcome your support, feedback, and contributions.</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z"></path></svg>Tip</p><p dir="auto"><strong>Don't want to set up a local environment?</strong> Use the hosted version at <strong><a href="https://sprite.gorest.ai/" rel="nofollow">sprite.gorest.ai</a></strong> — sign in and generate game-ready, transparent spritesheets directly in your browser. No installation, no API keys, no configuration.</p>
</div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Tip for Codex users:</strong> You do not need to connect an extra API to customize this tool. Open this repo in Codex and point it at this document or the codebase, then describe what you want to change. Codex can update the software directly.</p>
<p dir="auto">This is a combination of dynamic software and a no-UI workflow: instead of operating fixed controls inside an interface, you describe the tool or workflow you need, and the agent changes the tool to match.</p>
<p dir="auto">For durable agent instructions and prompt templates, see <a href="/NO6KIKO/gorest-2d-animation-spritesheet-generator/blob/main/AGENTS.md"><code>AGENTS.md</code></a> and <a href="/NO6KIKO/gorest-2d-animation-spritesheet-generator/blob/main/docs/AGENT_WORKFLOW.md"><code>docs/AGENT_WORKFLOW.md</code></a>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Recommended model:</strong> use GPT-5.5 (<code>gpt-5.5</code>) for this workflow. It is a strong fit for coding-heavy, tool-heavy, long-context agent work like generating assets, editing scene metadata, and modifying the app itself.</p>
</blockquote>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Spritesheet Playback</h3><a id="user-content-spritesheet-playback" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Spritesheet Playback" href="#spritesheet-playback"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/NO6KIKO/gorest-2d-animation-spritesheet-generator/blob/main/docs/readme/spritesheet-walk-preview.jpg"><img src="/NO6KIKO/gorest-2d-animation-spritesheet-generator/raw/main/docs/readme/spritesheet-walk-preview.jpg" alt="Spritesheet playback preview for a side-walk cycle" width="900" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
</p>
<p dir="auto">The app is designed for fast visual iteration: open a scene, place the character and props, inspect every spritesheet used in that scene, tune metadata, preview movement, and save the finished setup.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Codex-Assisted Animation Agent</h2><a id="user-content-codex-assisted-animation-agent" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Codex-Assisted Animation Agent" href="#codex-assisted-animation-agent"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The core idea is to vibe-code a no-manual-pipeline 2D animation agent without relying on a video generation model.</p>
<p dir="auto">Instead of manually operating a traditional animation stack, you can prompt Codex, upload a reference image, and let Codex call the local agent to produce a game-ready 2D spritesheet. The app then plays the animation directly from that spritesheet in a live preview, so the result can be checked immediately and used in a game engine.</p>
<p dir="auto">The workflow is broader than character animation:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Codex can generate or import spritesheets.</li>
<li>Codex can place animated sprites onto a background like a scene-compositing tool.</li>
<li>Codex can configure layers, metadata, trigger settings, scene state, and preview behavior.</li>
<li>Codex can revise the product framework itself while the tool evolves.</li>
<li>The browser becomes the visual result surface, while Codex handles setup, editing, and iteration.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">This is meant to reduce or skip the manual Spine-style workflow for early game prototyping: prompt, generate, composite, preview, adjust, and save.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What You Can Do</h2><a id="user-content-what-you-can-do" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What You Can Do" href="#what-you-can-do"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Create and save multiple scenes.</li>
<li>Arrange scene cards on the <code>2D Canvas</code> as a scrollable freeform flow board.</li>
<li>Use scene-card shortcuts on the <code>2D Canvas</code>: <code>Ctrl+C</code>, <code>Ctrl+V</code>, <code>Ctrl+D</code>, <code>Ctrl+X</code>, <code>Delete</code>, <code>Backspace</code>, <code>Ctrl+Z</code>, and <code>Ctrl+Y</code>.</li>
<li>Right-click scene cards for copy, cut, paste, duplicate, and delete actions.</li>
<li>Place backgrounds, characters, props, UI elements, and animated sprites as editable layers.</li>
<li>Drag, resize, scale, reorder, hide, and show scene layers.</li>
<li>Preview a playable side-scroller scene with character movement.</li>
<li>Import or generate 12-frame and 16-frame spritesheets.</li>
<li>Composite animated sprites directly onto scene backgrounds.</li>
<li>Open the <code>Spritesheets</code> page to see every animation clip used by the current scene.</li>
<li>Edit spritesheet metadata such as asset role, clip name, trigger type, game state, direction, loop mode, and tags.</li>
<li>Let Codex configure scenes, layers, assets, and animation metadata from prompts.</li>
<li>Let Codex modify the tool's product framework as the workflow changes.</li>
<li>Save reusable assets and scenes into the local game asset library.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">The current local workflow does not require an API key. If you later enable cloud image generation, copy the example env file and add your own key:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cp .env.example .env.local"><pre>cp .env.example .env.local</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">On Windows PowerShell:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Copy-Item .env.example .env.local"><pre><span class="pl-c1">Copy-Item</span> .env.example .env.local</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Creator Workflow</h2><a id="user-content-creator-workflow" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Creator Workflow" href="#creator-workflow"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This project is organized around a game-scene authoring workflow rather than a single character animation tool. A typical production pass looks like this:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Start from the <code>Scenes</code> tab.
Create a new scene, duplicate an existing one, delete test scenes, or open a saved scene from the scene library. The <code>2D Canvas</code> view works as a freeform scene-flow board, so cards can be moved, resized, copied, pasted, duplicated, cut, deleted, and undone independently from layer edits inside an individual scene.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Build the scene base in the <code>Scene</code> tab.
Add a background first, then place the player character, props, UI objects, foreground objects, animated props, and effects as separate editable layers.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Arrange layers like a 2D composition.
Drag objects on the canvas, resize them with handles, adjust scale, and use the layer list to decide what appears above or below other objects.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Keep animation assets reusable.
Save finished characters, props, UI elements, and effects into the local asset library so the same asset can be inserted into future scenes.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Inspect all animation clips in <code>Spritesheets</code>.
The <code>Spritesheets</code> page lists every spritesheet used in the current scene, not just the main character. Use it to preview, locate, download, and edit each animation clip.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Tune each clip as game data.
Each spritesheet can carry metadata such as action name, direction, loop mode, trigger type, trigger value, game state, and tags. This makes the visual asset reusable as gameplay data later.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Preview the scene in <code>Action</code>.
Use the playable preview to check character movement, animation timing, layer order, lighting, scale, and scene composition.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Save the completed scene.
Save the scene once the background, character, props, spritesheets, and metadata feel correct.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Prompt Examples for Codex</h2><a id="user-content-prompt-examples-for-codex" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Prompt Examples for Codex" href="#prompt-examples-for-codex"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">These prompts are written for Codex. They describe the scene, the spritesheet, the visual mood, and the exact editor changes Codex should make.</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Create a new side-scroller scene in the asset library. The scene should be a refined Chinese-horror Shinto-style wooden hut interior with a long wooden table in the foreground. Add a separate animated prop layer on the table: a round glass fishbowl spritesheet with a realistic fantail goldfish swimming inside. Use a dark, polished, painterly horror mood, not a simple cartoon style. Save the background PNG, the spritesheet PNG, and wire the new scene into public/generated/game_asset_library.json."><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>Create a new side-scroller scene in the asset library. The scene should be a refined Chinese-horror Shinto-style wooden hut interior with a long wooden table in the foreground. Add a separate animated prop layer on the table: a round glass fishbowl spritesheet with a realistic fantail goldfish swimming inside. Use a dark, polished, painterly horror mood, not a simple cartoon style. Save the background PNG, the spritesheet PNG, and wire the new scene into public/generated/game_asset_library.json.
</code></pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Spritesheet Authoring Rules</h2><a id="user-content-spritesheet-authoring-rules" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Spritesheet Authoring Rules" href="#spritesheet-authoring-rules"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For stable 2D game animation, spritesheets should be treated as one complete animation sheet first, then split into frames. This avoids the common problem where individually generated frames change size, drift, or lose visual consistency.</p>
<p dir="auto">Recommended rules:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Generate or import a full spritesheet whenever possible, then split it into frames.</li>
<li>Keep every frame the same pixel size.</li>
<li>Keep the character or prop centered in a consistent frame box.</li>
<li>Preserve the original character proportions from the reference image.</li>
<li>Keep the feet or base anchor stable so the object does not slide around during playback.</li>
<li>Use small, close frame-to-frame changes for idle and breathing animations.</li>
<li>Use one clean walk cycle per sheet, then loop it in playback instead of repeating the same step too many times inside the sheet.</li>
<li>For side-scrollers, create separate clips for <code>idle</code>, <code>walk_right</code>, <code>walk_left</code>, and any special action.</li>
<li>Store direction, loop, trigger, and game-state metadata on the clip so it can be reused by scene logic.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Interaction Design Model</h2><a id="user-content-interaction-design-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Interaction Design Model" href="#interaction-design-model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Interactive scenes are easiest to manage when interaction behavior is separated from visual layers. The recommended structure is:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Interaction zone: a draggable and resizable area that decides where the player can interact.</li>
<li>Trigger condition: proximity, mouse click, keyboard input, inventory requirement, or scene-state requirement.</li>
<li>Action: show text, play an animation, hide or show a layer, give an item, switch scenes, or update game state.</li>
<li>State: a small key-value table such as <code>has_key</code>, <code>radio_on</code>, <code>door_open</code>, or <code>visited_bedroom</code>.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Useful presets for future scene building:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>Inspect</code>: show an eye prompt near an object, then display text when clicked.</li>
<li><code>Pickup</code>: collect an item and hide the scene object.</li>
<li><code>Toggle</code>: switch a layer, animation, or state on and off.</li>
<li><code>Door / Scene Link</code>: move from one scene to another.</li>
<li><code>Animated Prop</code>: play or loop a prop animation.</li>
<li><code>Conditional</code>: choose different results based on scene state or inventory.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Scene Framing</h2><a id="user-content-scene-framing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Scene Framing" href="#scene-framing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The editor supports a scene-size mindset instead of only a single image-size mindset. Use this when designing for different targets:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Set the full scene width and height for long side-scrolling spaces.</li>
<li>Set the viewport frame separately for desktop, tablet, or phone-style previews.</li>
<li>Position the background inside the viewport to test what the player sees first.</li>
<li>Keep gameplay objects in world coordinates so the player can move through a larger scene.</li>
<li>Use foreground and background layers to create depth while keeping the game readable.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Included Scene Flow Example</h2><a id="user-content-included-scene-flow-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Included Scene Flow Example" href="#included-scene-flow-example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The current sample library includes a horror Shinto shrine entrance scene for a side-scroller prototype. It uses a wide shrine-gate background with a foreground incense burner, a talking Wei Yang close-up spritesheet, and editable layer metadata so the scene can be adjusted directly in the browser.</p>
<p dir="auto">It also includes a Shinto-style wooden hut interior scene with a refined horror tabletop fishbowl spritesheet. The fishbowl is stored as a reusable animated prop, so it can be inspected from the <code>Spritesheets</code> page and reused in other scenes.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Spritesheet Page</h2><a id="user-content-spritesheet-page" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Spritesheet Page" href="#spritesheet-page"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>Spritesheets</code> page is a scene-level animation library. It lists each spritesheet clip currently attached to scene layers, including character animations, animated props, UI animations, and imported assets.</p>
<p dir="auto">Each card supports:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>Preview</code>: play that clip in the frame preview.</li>
<li><code>Locate Layer</code>: jump back to the scene editor with the matching layer selected.</li>
<li><code>PNG</code>: download the underlying spritesheet image when available.</li>
<li><code>Edit</code>: change metadata for reusable confirmed assets.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Built-in scene kit assets can be previewed and layered. Persistent metadata editing is available for confirmed user assets.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Game Asset Library</h2><a id="user-content-game-asset-library" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Game Asset Library" href="#game-asset-library"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Saved assets and scenes are stored locally under:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="public/generated/game_asset_library.json"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>public/generated/game_asset_library.json
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Generated or imported PNG files are also stored in:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="public/generated/"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>public/generated/
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This makes the project easy to keep as a self-contained prototype. If you add large generated assets, review them before committing.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Available Scripts</h2><a id="user-content-available-scripts" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Available Scripts" href="#available-scripts"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm run dev"><pre>npm run dev</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Starts the local Express and Vite development server.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm run build"><pre>npm run build</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Builds the frontend and bundles the server into <code>dist/</code>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm run start"><pre>npm run start</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Runs the production build from <code>dist/</code>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm run lint"><pre>npm run lint</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Runs TypeScript checks without emitting files.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Notes</h2><a id="user-content-notes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Notes" href="#notes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>The app is local-first. Most editing and preview work happens without a cloud API.</li>
<li><code>.env.local</code> is ignored so private keys are not pushed to GitHub.</li>
<li>The scene editor is built for side-scrolling 2D game prototyping, with reusable layers, spritesheets, and scene states as the core model.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing</h2><a id="user-content-contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing" href="#contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We welcome contributions to the Gorest 2D Animation Spritesheet Generator! If you would like to help improve the project, please follow these steps:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Fork the repository</strong>: Click the 'Fork' button at the top right of the page.</li>
<li><strong>Clone your fork</strong>: Use <code>git clone https://github.com/YOUR_USERNAME/gorest-2d-animation-spritesheet-generator.git</code> to clone your fork locally.</li>
<li><strong>Create a branch</strong>: Create a new branch for your changes with <code>git checkout -b my-feature-branch</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Make your changes</strong>: Implement your feature or bug fix.</li>
<li><strong>Commit your changes</strong>: Use <code>git commit -m 'Add some feature'</code> to commit your changes.</li>
<li><strong>Push to your fork</strong>: Push your changes back to your fork with <code>git push origin my-feature-branch</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Open a pull request</strong>: Go to the original repository and click on 'New Pull Request'.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Thank you for your contributions!</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">FAQ</h2><a id="user-content-faq" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: FAQ" href="#faq"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Q: How do I install the project?</strong><br>
A: Follow the 'Quick Start' section in the README to install Node.js, clone the repository, and install dependencies.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Q: Can I use this project for commercial purposes?</strong><br>
A: The source code is available under the MIT License. The included media assets are demo/project preview materials and are not licensed for separate commercial reuse unless explicitly stated otherwise.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Q: I'm not comfortable setting up a local dev environment. Is there an easier way?</strong><br>
A: Yes. The hosted version at <a href="https://sprite.gorest.ai/" rel="nofollow">sprite.gorest.ai</a> runs entirely in the browser — sign in and start generating spritesheets without installing anything.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Q: How can I report a bug?</strong><br>
A: Please open an issue in the GitHub repository with a detailed description of the bug and steps to reproduce it.</p>

    <div id="js-global-screen-reader-notice-assertive" class="sr-only mt-n1" aria-live="assertive" aria-atomic="true"></div>
  </body>
</html>
